> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/announcement-video

        Idea:
Refactor the video editor to be elegant, well-structured, and aligned with the reigh-app architecture at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app.

CURRENT STATE OF VIDEO EDITOR:
- App.tsx is 1325 lines (god component handling everything)
- App.css is 1224 lines (one giant file, no scoping)
- State management is useState/useRef chains in App.tsx
- No tests, dead code (DebugTrack, DEBUG_MODE, LivePreview.tsx)
- ~3745 lines in viewer/src, ~1435 lines in shared/

REIGH-APP ARCHITECTURE TO ALIGN WITH:
- Vite + React 18 + TypeScript (same stack)
- Tailwind CSS + shadcn-ui components (NOT plain CSS)
- React Query for server state, Context for global UI state
- Feature-based directory structure: tools/[feature]/pages|components|hooks|settings
- Path aliases (@/* -> src/*)
- No CSS files - pure Tailwind utility classes
- Supabase for backend (auth, db, storage, realtime)
- Existing video components: StyledVideoPlayer, VideoPortionTimeline, VideoPortionEditor
- Task system for async operations (like rendering)
- Settings persistence via useAutoSaveSettings
- @dnd-kit for drag-and-drop
- Konva for canvas rendering
- lucide-react for icons

REFACTORING GOALS:

1. BREAK UP App.tsx into focused modules:
   - TimelineState context/hook (clip selection, track management, save/load)
   - TimelineEditor component (the timeline UI)
   - PreviewPanel component (Remotion player + overlay editor)
   - PropertiesPanel component (ClipPanel + TrackPanel in sidebar)
   - Toolbar component (add track, add text, render, zoom controls)

2. REPLACE App.css with Tailwind:
   - Convert all CSS to Tailwind utility classes
   - Use shadcn-ui components where applicable (Button, Select, Slider, Popover, Card, etc.)
   - Remove the CSS file entirely

3. STATE MANAGEMENT aligned with reigh-app:
   - TimelineContext for editor state (selected clip, selected track, current time)
   - React Query for loading/saving timeline.json (with proper query keys)
   - useAutoSaveSettings pattern for editor preferences (zoom level, collapsed sections)

4. DIRECTORY STRUCTURE matching reigh-app's tool pattern:
   tools/video-editor/
     pages/VideoEditorPage.tsx
     components/
       TimelineEditor/
       PreviewPanel/
       PropertiesPanel/
       Toolbar/
       TrackLabel/
       ClipAction/
     hooks/
       useTimelineState.ts
       useTimelineSync.ts
       useCrossTrackDrag.ts
       useOverlayEditor.ts
     contexts/
       TimelineContext.tsx
     lib/
       timeline-data.ts
       coordinate-utils.ts

5. CLEAN UP:
   - Remove dead code (DebugTrack, DEBUG_MODE, LivePreview.tsx)
   - Remove viewer/.gitignore (use root .gitignore)
   - Clean up unused imports and files
   - Add proper TypeScript strict typing throughout

6. COMPONENT LIBRARY alignment:
   - Use shadcn Button, Select, Slider, Popover, Card, Input components
   - Use lucide-react icons instead of emoji (play/pause/skip buttons)
   - Use @dnd-kit for drag-and-drop instead of custom pointer event handlers
   - Use Tailwind for all styling

CONSTRAINTS:
- Keep the shared/ compositions layer intact (it works well)
- Keep timeline.json schema unchanged
- Keep the Remotion Player integration
- Keep all current functionality working
- This is a refactor, not a feature change

  "meta_commentary": "## Execution Guidance\n\n### Highest-Risk Task: T8 (@dnd-kit spike)\nThis is the make-or-break decision point. useCrossTrackDrag.ts deeply hooks into react-timeline-editor's internal DOM (setPointerCapture on .clip-action elements, cloneNode for ghosts, getBoundingClientRect on .timeline-editor-edit-row). The timeline library already owns primary drag handling via its onActionMoveStart/Moving/MoveEnd callbacks. @dnd-kit's DndContext will try to own pointer events too. Start with a minimal spike: wrap a single clip-action div in useDraggable, add one track row as useDroppable, and test if the two systems conflict. If events fight, STOP and keep useCrossTrackDrag.ts \u2014 the rest of the refactor is unaffected.\n\n### Second Highest Risk: T5 (State extraction)\nApp.tsx has ~25 useState/useRef declarations and ~15 callback functions with complex interdependencies (e.g., commitData calls materializeData which reads dataRef, scheduleSave reads lastSavedSignature via closure). Extract carefully \u2014 the closure chain must be preserved. Test save/load after every incremental move.\n\n### Parallelism\n- T1, T3, T4 can run in parallel (infrastructure, alias, cleanup \u2014 independent)\n- T9, T10, T11 can run in parallel after T5 (component extractions from different parts of App.tsx)\n- T14, T15, T16 can run in parallel after their respective component tasks (Tailwind conversions)\n\n### Tailwind 3.x vs v4 Gotcha\nThe plan explicitly requires Tailwind 3.4.x (PostCSS-based config). Do NOT install Tailwind v4 which uses a completely different CSS-first approach. The viewer uses Vite 7.3.1 \u2014 verify PostCSS integration works with this Vite version (Vite 7 may need @tailwindcss/vite or similar).\n\n### shadcn + React 19 Compatibility\nThe viewer uses React 19.2. shadcn-ui's latest version should support this, but verify during T2 installation. If shadcn CLI complains about React version, use --force or check for a React 19-compatible shadcn version.\n\n### CSS Variable Bridge\nThe existing Catppuccin theme uses CSS custom properties (--ctp-base, --ctp-surface0, etc.) in App.css :root. These need to be bridged to both: (1) Tailwind theme config (so bg-surface0 etc. work), and (2) shadcn's expected CSS variables (--background, --foreground, --card, --primary, etc.). This bridge is critical for visual consistency during the migration.\n\n### Incremental CSS Removal\nDuring Phase 5 (T14-T17), remove CSS from App.css incrementally as each component is converted. Don't remove CSS selectors until the component using them has been fully converted to Tailwind. T17 does the final deletion.\n\n### The settings/ Gap\nThe original plan omitted useAutoSaveSettings. T6 was added to address the gate flag. It should be a lightweight hook wrapping localStorage with JSON serialization and a debounced write \u2014 don't over-engineer it. The reigh-app pattern likely uses a similar approach.\n\n### File Move Strategy for T18\nUse git mv for all file moves so git tracks the renames. Do all moves in one commit to keep the diff clean. Update all imports AFTER moving files (IDE auto-import won't help since paths change)."
}

        Note: User explicitly approved this plan at the gate checkpoint.
        Robustness level: standard.

        Requirements:
        - Implement the intent, not just the text.
        - Adapt if repository reality contradicts the plan.
        - Report deviations explicitly.
        - Output concrete files changed and commands run.
        - Use the tasks in `finalize.json` as the execution boundary. Do not create or rewrite tracking artifacts directly.
        - Return `task_updates` with one object per completed or skipped task: `{"task_id": "...", "status": "done|skipped", "executor_notes": "..."}`.
        - Keep `executor_notes` specific enough that a reviewer can map each update to the actual code and commands you ran.

        Sense checks to keep in mind during execution (reviewer will verify these):
- SC1 (T1): Does npm run dev start successfully with Tailwind 3.4.x loaded alongside existing App.css? Are Tailwind utility classes applying correctly when added to a test element?
- SC2 (T2): Do shadcn components exist at viewer/src/shared/components/ui/ and are they importable via @/shared/components/ui/button etc.? Does npm run build pass with all new dependencies?
- SC3 (T3): Can you import a file using @/tools/video-editor/... and have it resolve correctly in both dev server and TypeScript compilation?
- SC4 (T4): Is DEBUG_MODE and DebugTrack import removed from RemotionPreview.tsx? Is viewer/.gitignore deleted? Does npm run build pass cleanly?
- SC5 (T5): Does the app render identically after state extraction? Can you: select a clip, edit a property, save (verify POST /api/save-timeline fires), reload and see saved changes? Is there any state that was missed in the extraction?
- SC6 (T6): Do editor settings (scaleWidth, collapsed sections, asset panel filter) persist to localStorage and survive a full page reload? Was the ad hoc localStorage in AssetPanel.tsx replaced?
- SC7 (T7): Does the React Query migration preserve: auto-refresh on external changes, debounced save, signature comparison to prevent overwrites, and immediate asset appearance after upload (via invalidateQueries)?
- SC8 (T8): Did the @dnd-kit spike succeed or fail? If succeeded: does cross-track drag work without conflicting with react-timeline-editor's internal drag? If failed: is useCrossTrackDrag.ts preserved unchanged?
- SC9 (T9): Does the extracted Toolbar render all controls (render button, zoom, save status, timecode, add track/text) and do they all function correctly via context?
- SC10 (T10): Does the Remotion Player still play/pause/seek correctly? Does overlay editing (drag handles on text/image overlays) still work in the extracted PreviewPanel?
- SC11 (T11): Does clip selection in timeline populate the ClipPanel? Do property edits (volume, trim, speed) save correctly? Does asset drag-to-timeline still work from the AssetPanel?
- SC12 (T12): Does the timeline render all tracks and clips? Can clips be: dragged horizontally, resized, split (S key), deleted, muted (M key)? Do keyboard shortcuts (Space, Alt+Up/Down, Esc) work? Does the TrackSettingsPopover open correctly?
- SC13 (T13): Is App.tsx under 100 lines? Does the full editor work end-to-end: load, drag, resize, split, mute, cross-track drag, overlay edit, asset drop, all keyboard shortcuts, render, auto-save?
- SC14 (T14): Does the Toolbar use shadcn Button and lucide-react icons? Are all CSS classes removed from the Toolbar section of App.css? Does the dark theme still look correct?
- SC15 (T15): Does the preview area use Tailwind utilities and shadcn Card? Are play/pause icons from lucide-react? Do overlay editor drag handles still render and function?
- SC16 (T16): Are all form elements in ClipPanel using shadcn components (Input, Select, Slider, Button, Collapsible)? Do dropdowns (effect type, blend mode, etc.) open and select correctly? Do sliders (volume, opacity, speed) respond to input?
- SC17 (T17): Is App.css deleted? Does timeline-editor-overrides.css exist with ~50 lines of library overrides? Does the full layout render correctly with Tailwind utilities? Is the dark theme consistent?
- SC18 (T18): Does the final structure match tools/video-editor/{pages,components,hooks,contexts,lib,settings}/? Is App.tsx minimal (renders VideoEditorPage)? Do all @/ imports resolve? Does npm run build pass with zero errors?
Watch items to keep visible during execution:
- CRITICAL: @dnd-kit vs react-timeline-editor coexistence (T8). The timeline library owns pointer events on its internal DOM (.timeline-editor-edit-area, .ReactVirtualized__Grid). useCrossTrackDrag.ts hooks into these same elements with setPointerCapture and cloned DOM ghosts. A spike MUST validate feasibility before committing. If spike fails, keep useCrossTrackDrag.ts and defer @dnd-kit.
- Catppuccin CSS variable mapping: App.css defines CSS custom properties for the dark theme. These must be mapped to Tailwind theme tokens AND shadcn's CSS custom properties (--background, --foreground, --primary, etc.) so shadcn components render in the existing palette, not default shadcn colors.
- Tailwind version: Must be 3.4.x with PostCSS/autoprefixer (matching reigh-app), NOT Tailwind v4 CSS-first. The config approach is fundamentally different between v3 and v4.
- Asset panel ad hoc localStorage (AssetPanel.tsx:18-27): This existing persistence must be migrated to the new useEditorSettings hook in T6, not left as-is.
- react-timeline-editor library override CSS: ~50 lines targeting internal class names with !important cannot be converted to Tailwind. These MUST be preserved in a scoped CSS file (timeline-editor-overrides.css).
- The lastSavedSignature comparison (prevents overwriting external changes) must survive the React Query migration in T7. This is a critical safety mechanism.
- Vite dev server middleware (vite.config.ts, 446 lines) stays as-is — it provides /api/timeline, /api/save-timeline, /api/upload, etc. Do not refactor it as part of this work.
- shared/ directory is NOT touched by this refactor. All @shared/* imports continue to work unchanged.
- shadcn components.json must configure aliases.ui to @/shared/components/ui (not @/components/ui) to match reigh-app pattern.
- The viewer uses Vite 7.3.1 and React 19.2 — verify shadcn-ui and Tailwind 3.4.x compatibility with these versions before installing.
- settings/ directory omission was flagged — T6 and T18 address this. Ensure useEditorSettings is created and settings/ appears in final structure.
- PropertiesPanel boundary: plan keeps current asset+clip sidebar shape. The user's original idea mentioned ClipPanel+TrackPanel — current plan is acceptable per gate review but note the minor flag.

     title={renderLog || undefined}\n+        >\n+          <Plus className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n+          {renderStatus === \"rendering\" ? \"Rendering...\" : \"Render\"}\n+        </Button>\n+      </div>\n+    </div>\n+  );\n+}\n*** End Patch\n"
